Motivation: 光锥分布振幅（LCDAs）对精确唯象研究至关重要，而通过大动量有效理论（LaMET）从格点QCD提取LCDAs需依赖准分布振幅（quasi-DAs），但其因子化存在1/P_h幂次修正（含靶质量与高扭度效应），尤其对重子系统尚缺乏系统分析。

Method: 基于动量矩关系建立quasi-DA与LCDA的严格联系，利用算符乘积展开（OPE）与重整化群分析，推导靶质量修正的全阶闭式解析表达式；结合格点数据对Λ重子进行数值拟合，并显式构造下一领头扭度（twist-4）算符以刻画高扭度修正。

Result: 获得领头扭度下重子quasi-DA靶质量修正的全阶闭式解；数值表明该修正随重子动量增大快速压低，在端点区可忽略；给出twist-4算符的具体形式，完成quasi-DA因子化的完整幂次结构。

Conclusion: 本文为重子LCDAs的LaMET格点提取提供了首个系统性幂次修正框架，特别是靶质量修正的精确处理将显著提升Λ等重子分布振幅的精度，亦适用于重味重子及准横动量依赖分布（quasi-TMDs）。

Abstract: Light-cone distribution amplitudes (LCDAs) are essential to precision phenomenological studies. They can be accessed from lattice QCD through the large-momentum effective theory (LaMET) via quasi-distribution amplitudes (quasi-DAs). Factorization of quasi-DAs receive power corrections in inverse powers of the hadron momentum, including target-mass and higher-twist corrections. In this work, we present the first systematic analysis of such power corrections for the leading-twist baryon quasi-DA. Establishing the moment relation between the quasi-DA and the LCDA, we derive an exact closed-form relation that resums target-mass correction to all orders at leading twist. This result also applies to heavy baryons and to quasi-transverse-momentum-dependent distributions. We numerically assess these corrections for the $Λ$ baryon quasi-DA using existing lattice data, finding that the target-mass correction decreases rapidly with increasing baryon momentum and is almost negligible in the endpoint regions. In addition, we explicitly construct the next-to-leading-twist operators entering the quasi-DA factorization. Our results are a first step toward quantifying the power corrections in future lattice determinations of light or heavy baryon LCDAs.

</details>


### [98] [Taming lepton portal dark matter by a non-invertible selection rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29051)
*Junichiro Kawamura*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用非可逆对称性（non-invertible symmetry）作为选择定则，抑制轻子门户暗物质模型中的味破坏效应，同时兼容PMNS矩阵的大混合角；构造了一个基于Z₂规范化的Z₅对称性的简单模型，实现单味主导的门户耦合，并讨论了I型跷跷板补全中的味泄漏及Z玻色子衰变中轻子味非普适性的实验探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决轻子门户暗物质模型中因标准模型轻子味结构与暗物质耦合导致的强味破坏问题，该问题在传统可逆对称性（如U(1)、Z_N）下无法兼顾大PMNS混合角与对味破坏的压制。

Method: 引入非可逆选择定则（non-invertible selection rule），构造一个由Z₅全局对称性经Z₂ gauging得到的非可逆对称性结构；结合味空间的离散对称性破缺模式，约束轻子-暗物质门户耦合矩阵的结构；在type-I seesaw框架下分析重右手中微子诱导的味泄漏效应；计算Z→ℓᵢℓⱼ衰变中LFU偏离（R_K, R_π等可观测量）的理论预期。

Result: 证明可逆对称性无法同时满足PMNS大角与味安全；给出Z₂-gauged Z₅模型可自然实现单味主导耦合（e.g., only τ-coupling allowed），且与中微子振荡数据一致；预言Z玻色子衰变中存在可观测的轻子味非普适性信号（δR_{ℓℓ'} ∼ 10⁻³–10⁻²），可被Belle II或未来LHCb/HL-LHC实验检验。

Conclusion: 非可逆对称性为轻子门户暗物质提供了一种新型味保护机制，突破了传统可逆对称性的限制；该框架将暗物质物理、中微子质量起源与高精度轻子味物理统一于同一对称性结构，具备可证伪性。

Abstract: We point out that a non-invertible selection rule can control the flavor violation inherent in lepton portal dark matter, a weakly interacting massive particle candidate that can evade severe constraints from direct detection experiments. We show that ordinary invertible symmetries cannot suppress flavor-violating portal couplings while accommodating the large mixing angles in the PMNS matrix. We then present a simple construction based on the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauging of a $\mathbb{Z}_5$ symmetry, which realizes one-flavor dominance of the portal couplings consistently with the PMNS mixing matrix. We also discuss possible flavor leakage in a type-I seesaw completion and study experimental probes of the model through lepton observables, especially lepton flavor non-universality in $Z$ boson decays.

</details>


### [99] [Higgs boson pair production in SMEFT: shape and truncation studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29132)
*Manosch Bär,Bakar Chargeishvili,Ramona Gröber,Gudrun Heinrich,Konstantin Schmid*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了SMEFT中维度-6 Wilson系数（含圈压低项）对双Higgs产生截面的影响，包含NLO QCD修正，并基于Wilson系数的多项式参数化分析微分分布（如m_hh和p_T,h），识别出导致显著形状畸变的基准场景；发现部分场景在线性截断下仍物理合理，而另一些则因忽略二次维度-6贡献而产生非物理微分截面，揭示大反常耦合下线性近似的失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验SMEFT维度-6算符（含圈压低项）对双Higgs产生可观测量的影响，评估线性有效场论展开的适用边界，尤其关注微分分布对Wilson系数高阶依赖的敏感性。

Method: 采用NLO QCD精度的SMEFT计算框架，将双Higgs截面参数化为Wilson系数的多项式（含线性与二次项），系统扫描各维度-6算符组合，重点分析m_hh与p_T,h微分分布的形状变化及物理性约束。

Result: 识别出若干使m_hh和p_T,h分布发生显著形状畸变的Wilson系数基准点；其中部分点在线性近似下保持微分截面正定，而另一些点在仅保留线性项时导致负截面或非物理行为，表明二次维度-6贡献不可忽略。

Conclusion: 双Higgs微分分布是探测SMEFT高阶Wilson系数效应的灵敏探针；线性SMEFT截断在大反常耦合区域失效，必须纳入二次维度-6项以保证理论自洽性与观测解释的可靠性。

Abstract: We present a comparative study of the influence of Wilson coefficients on Higgs boson pair production cross sections in SMEFT up to dimension-6, also including loop-suppressed operators. Our study accounts for NLO QCD corrections, and is based on updated parametrizations of the cross section as a polynomial in the Wilson coefficients. We focus on differential distributions for the Higgs boson pair invariant mass, as well as the transverse momenta of the leading Higgs bosons, identifying benchmark scenarios exhibiting pronounced shape distortions in the $m_{hh}$ and $p_{T,h}$ distributions. While some of these scenarios remain physically meaningful under a purely linear truncation of the SMEFT expansion, others yield unphysical differential cross sections when quadratic dimension-6 contributions are omitted, exposing the breakdown of the linear approximation for large anomalous couplings.

</details>


### [100] [Probing strange-sector flavor-changing neutral currents at DUNE-like facilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29178)
*Xin-Shuai Yan,Miao Liu,Qin Chang,Ya-Dong Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究DUNE类实验设施通过包含性深度非弹性中微子散射对下夸克-奇异夸克味改变中性流（FCNC）相互作用的探测灵敏度；在Dirac与Majorana中微子通用低能有效拉格朗日框架下，给出近/远探测器对无量纲Wilson系数（以费米常数GF为基准）的投影限制，近探测器达O(10⁻³–10⁻²)，远探测器弱约一个数量级；系统误差中，近探测器受限于背景率不确定性，远探测器则受限于信号率不确定性；远探测器界限对中微子质量序与δCP的依赖主要体现在含电子味的系数上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测超出标准模型的味改变中性流（FCNC）相互作用，特别是ds耦合，是检验新物理的重要途径；DUNE实验凭借其高亮度宽谱束流与双探测器构型，为该过程提供独特探针。

Method: 基于通用低能有效场论（EFT）框架，分别处理Dirac与Majorana中微子情形；采用inclusive deep-inelastic scattering截面计算，结合DUNE近/远探测器几何、效率与本底模型；对CP优化与τ优化束流配置进行蒙特卡洛模拟与统计分析，纳入系统误差协方差矩阵，使用似然拟合提取Wilson系数90% CL上限。

Result: 近探测器对|C^{ds}_V|等Wilson系数的90%置信度上限达3×10⁻³–2×10⁻²量级（GF单位）；远探测器对应限弱约10倍；系统误差主导下，近端极限由背景率不确定性（~5%）主导，远端由信号率不确定性（~10%）主导；远端界限对ν质量序与δCP的依赖仅显著影响含e味的算符（如C^{de}_V），其余ds主导项基本无关。

Conclusion: DUNE近探测器是当前最强的ds-FCNC探针之一，其灵敏度进入TeV尺度新物理典型EFT参数空间；远探测器虽受限于统计与系统误差，但仍可提供正交约束，尤其对轻子味结构敏感；结果凸显近探测器在BSM中性流搜索中的不可替代性，并为未来EFT全局拟合提供关键输入。

Abstract: We investigate the sensitivity of DUNE-like facilities to flavor-changing
  neutral-current interactions between down and strange quarks through inclusive
  deep-inelastic neutrino scattering. In the framework of a general low-energy
  effective Lagrangian for Dirac and Majorana neutrinos, we evaluate projected
  sensitivities for CP- and $τ$-optimized beam configurations. The strongest
  bounds on the dimensionless Wilson coefficients, defined relative to the Fermi
  constant $G_F$, reach $\mathcal{O}(10^{-3})\text{--}\mathcal{O}(10^{-2})$ at the
  near detector, whereas far-detector limits are roughly one order of magnitude
  weaker. When systematic uncertainties are included, the near-detector
  sensitivity is dominated by the background-rate uncertainty, while the
  far-detector sensitivity is limited primarily by the signal-rate uncertainty.
  At the far detector,
  the dependence of the projected bounds on the neutrino mass ordering and the Dirac
  CP-violating phase $δ_{\mathrm{CP}}$ is largely confined to coefficients
  involving the electron flavor.

</details>


### [101] [Embedding dependence of fermion mass hierarchies in $S_3$-symmetric Yukawa sectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29259)
*Ye Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在S₃家族对称性下，当不可约表示以多重性出现时，Yukawa耦合张量在生成空间中的嵌入自由度对其谱（即奇异值/质量）的影响；特别分析了复对称三族张量的奇异值结构，发现嵌入参数可显著放宽质量等级限制，并指出某Clifford代数构造因固定嵌入而无法再现轻子质量等级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当家族对称性群（如S₃）作用于费米子代空间V时，若Yukawa张量所处的不变子空间具有非平凡多重性（如Sym²V中1⊕2出现两次），则其具体嵌入方式未被对称性唯一确定——该几何自由度可能深刻影响可观测的质量谱，但此前未被系统研究。

Method: 运用表示论分解Sym²V，参数化所有S₃-不变Yukawa子空间L_{t,κ} ≅ ℂℙ¹×ℂℙ¹；通过求解逆奇异值问题（inverse SVD problem）刻画质量谱可行性条件；结合Clifford代数构造的具体嵌入，导出质量不等式约束。

Result: ① 在κ=0分支上，任意正有序三质量谱均可实现当且仅当允许嵌入自由变化；固定单态嵌入则引入有限等级上限；② L_{t,κ}含非零秩一矩阵 ⇔ 2tκ²=1，此时σ₃/σ₂无界；③ Clifford构造对应L_{2,0}，其奇异值满足m₁+m₂ ≤ m₃ ≤ m₁+3m₂，排除标准轻子质量比。

Conclusion: Yukawa张量的表示嵌入自由度是调控费米子质量等级的关键独立参数，其约束甚至强于对称性本身的表示内容；忽视该自由度将导致对模型唯象可行性的误判。

Abstract: When irreducible representations occur with multiplicity, a family symmetry fixes the representation content of a Yukawa sector but need not fix its embedding in the space of generation tensors. We study the spectral consequences of this additional embedding data for complex-symmetric three-family tensors with the $S_3$ assignment $V=\mathbf{1}\oplus\mathbf{2}$. Since $\operatorname{Sym}^2V=2\,\mathbf{1}\oplus2\,\mathbf{2}$, three-dimensional invariant Yukawa subspaces of type $\mathbf{1}\oplus\mathbf{2}$ form a continuous $\mathbb{CP}^1\times\mathbb{CP}^1$ family, denoted $\mathcal L_{t,κ}$ on a finite affine chart. On the $κ=0$ branch, we solve the inverse singular-value problem and obtain necessary and sufficient conditions for any prescribed ordered positive mass spectrum. A fixed singlet embedding imposes a finite hierarchy bound, whereas varying the embedding accommodates every positive three-family spectrum. For general embeddings, $\mathcal L_{t,κ}$ contains a nonzero rank-one matrix if and only if $2tκ^2=1$; equivalently, these are precisely the finite-chart embeddings for which $σ_3/σ_2$ is unbounded. In a conventional complex-symmetric $S_3$ three-Higgs sector, the same condition becomes $y_2y_5^2=\sqrt{2}\,y_1y_3^2$, so order-one Yukawa coupling ratios can support arbitrarily large mass hierarchies. We then apply the result to a recent Clifford-algebraic construction and show that its family-resolved Yukawa tensors span $\mathcal L_{2,0}$, whose singular values obey $m_1+m_2\leq m_3\leq m_1+3m_2$. This excludes the observed charged-lepton hierarchy for arbitrary complex vacuum alignment within the existing Higgs directions. The resulting obstruction is therefore tied to the fixed multiplicity embedding rather than to the $S_3$ representation content alone.

</details>


### [102] [Meson gravitational D-form factors and symmetry breaking in low-energy QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30434)
*Mamiya Kawaguchi,Kazuhiro Tanaka,Mitsuru Tanaka*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在三味线性σ模型中研究介子的引力D-型因子，揭示其与低能QCD对称性破缺的深刻联系；通过标量介子主导图像和手征极限分析，得到赝标量与标量介子D-型因子的解析表达式，并指出U(1)_A反常对标量及单态赝标量介子D-型因子的修正；现实味破缺下，低于1 GeV的标量介子质量谱相互作用显著影响其D-型因子；与含标度子场的手征微扰论对比表明，差异源于质量维数不同的相互作用项；D-型因子是探测低能强子物理对称性破缺结构的灵敏探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解低能QCD中对称性破缺机制，特别是手征对称性与标度对称性的实现方式，需借助介子在引力耦合下的形状信息——即D-型因子；该量编码能量-动量张量分布，对对称性破缺模式高度敏感。

Method: 采用三味线性σ模型框架，结合标量介子主导（SMD）图像，计算赝标量（π, K, η）与标量（a₀, f₀, σ）介子的引力D-型因子；在三味对称极限与手征极限下推导解析结果；引入显式味破缺项以再现标量介子质量谱；并与含动力学标度子的手征微扰论（χPT+σ）结果系统比较，聚焦相互作用项的质量维数差异。

Result: 在手征极限下，八重态赝标量介子D-型因子前向极限值严格为−1；U(1)_A反常导致单态赝标量η′与标量介子D-型因子出现非平凡修正；标量介子质量劈裂相互作用（如κ项）显著调制其D-型因子幅值与q²依赖；与χPT+σ对比显示，线性σ模型中维数为4的标量耦合项主导质量生成，而χPT中维数为2的导数耦合主导，造成D-型因子在红外行为上的本质差异。

Conclusion: 介子D-型因子是区分不同对称性破缺实现机制（如手征vs.标度破缺主导、线性vs.非线性实现）的关键可观测量；其q²依赖与前向极限值可定量约束标量扇区动力学及U(1)_A反常强度，为未来高亮度电子-离子对撞机（EIC）等实验提供理论基准。

Abstract: We investigate meson gravitational D-form factors in the three-flavor linear sigma model and their connection to symmetry breaking in low-energy QCD. Within the scalar meson dominance picture, we examine the scalar meson exchange contributions and their relation to meson masses through scalar-meson couplings. In the three-flavor symmetric limit, we first derive analytical expressions for the D-form factors of pseudoscalar and scalar mesons. In the chiral limit, the forward-limit value of the octet pseudoscalar D-form factor is fixed to $-1$, whereas the $U(1)_A$ anomaly generates additional contributions to the singlet pseudoscalar and scalar-meson D-form factors. For realistic flavor breaking, the interaction introduced to reproduce the scalar-meson mass hierarchy below $1$ GeV significantly affects the scalar-meson D-form factors. We further compare the linear sigma model results with those obtained from a chiral perturbation theory Lagrangian including a dilatonic scalar field and show that their differences are governed by the canonical mass dimensions of the interaction terms contributing to meson mass generation. These results indicate that meson D-form factors provide sensitive probes of symmetry-breaking structures in low-energy hadron physics.

</details>


### [103] [Probing Ultra-Compressed Scotogenic Dark Matter at the HL-LHC via 4D Spacetime Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29275)
*Renjie Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用HL-LHC精密计时探测器（30 ps时间分辨率）作为第四维度观测量，针对超压缩scotogenic模型中寿命在100–1000 mm对应的70–700 ps到达延迟的带电惰性标量衰变事例，发展一种‘4D时空追踪’策略；该策略基于低p_T消失轨迹stub匹配延迟时间信号，在无背景下实现12–36%信号效率，并预计在3000 fb⁻¹数据下以95% CL排除标量质量达≈670 GeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LHC位移轻子与消失轨迹搜寻对该模型（2 GeV质量劈裂、共湮灭满足暗物质遗存密度）接受度低且未优化；而其宏观衰变长度（100–1000 mm）对应可分辨的时间延迟（70–700 ps），远超标准模型本底的亚皮秒级延迟，为引入时间维度提供物理动机。

Method: 采用‘4D timing strategy’：将低横动量消失轨迹stub（disappearing-track stub）与精密计时探测器中延迟时间击中（delayed timing hit）进行匹配，不依赖完整轻子重建；利用cτ ∝ y⁻² 与中微子质量约束 y²λ₅ ∼ sub-eV² 的解耦，选取y使衰变长度落入最优计时窗口（100–1000 mm）。

Result: 在4.20×10⁶个模拟标准模型本底事件中观测到零背景；信号效率为12–36%（200 GeV基准点为14%）；在3000 fb⁻¹积分亮度、保守背景假设下，95%置信水平可排除标量质量达≈670 GeV。

Conclusion: 超压缩scotogenic模型中共湮灭走廊——传统上最难探测的参数空间——可通过HL-LHC的4D时空追踪（即结合三维空间+精确时间）高效覆盖；该策略不依赖于y和λ₅的唯一取值，仅需y足够小以满足时间分辨窗口，与辐射产生中微子质量机制相容且具可观测性。

Abstract: The ultra-compressed scotogenic regime, in which a 2\,GeV mass splitting between the charged inert scalar and the dark-matter fermion is motivated by and compatible with co-annihilation to the observed relic density for suitable choices of the remaining model parameters, produces decay leptons for which existing LHC displaced-lepton and disappearing-track searches retain only partial, unoptimised acceptance, leaving room for a dedicated timing-assisted strategy. We propose exploiting the 30\,ps timing resolution of the HL-LHC precision timing detectors as a fourth observable, converting the macroscopic scalar decay length of 100--1000\,mm into an arrival-time delay of 70--700\,ps---more than three orders of magnitude above the sub-picosecond delays of all prompt Standard Model backgrounds. Operating at the level of a low-$p_T$ disappearing-track stub matched to a delayed timing hit, without requiring full lepton reconstruction, our 4D timing strategy achieves signal efficiencies of 12--36\% across the probed mass range (14\% at the 200\,GeV benchmark) and zero background across $4.20\times10^6$ simulated prompt SM events, projecting 95\%\,C.L.\ exclusion up to scalar masses of $\simeq\!670$\,GeV at 3000\,fb$^{-1}$ under conservative background assumptions. The scalar lifetime is set by the Yukawa coupling $y$ through $cτ\propto y^{-2}$, while sub-eV neutrino masses constrain only the product $y^2λ_5$; the small couplings that place the charged scalar in the optimal timing window are therefore compatible with, though not uniquely fixed by, the radiative neutrino-mass mechanism, making the co-annihilation corridor---conventionally the most elusive regime---an accessible target for 4D spacetime tracking at the HL-LHC.

</details>


### [104] [Finite-Width Dissolution of Radial Spectroscopy in Single-Top Mesonic Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29476)
*Bing-Dong Wan,Shuo Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了含不稳定重夸克（如顶夸克）的束缚态系统在重质量展开下的宽度继承行为，指出其极点宽度主要继承组分夸克宽度，径向分裂则保持为Λ_rad量级；通过非厄米Bethe-Salpeter框架实现复极点质量，并验证了O(Λ²/m_Q²)压制与宽度继承的算符层面机制；结果表明：小宽度径向峰随宽度增大而消失，稳定顶夸克本征值仅作为参考极点存在，不形成可分辨多峰谱，但可能在Wbq末态中留下宽阈值增强等过程依赖的定性信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解顶夸克作为极重且短寿命粒子在强子化前如何影响潜在束缚态（如t\bar{q}）的谱学性质与衰变动力学，特别是其有限宽度对极点结构和径向激发的影响，这对高精度电弱与QCD唯象分析至关重要。

Method: 采用瞬时Bethe-Salpeter方程框架，引入复极点质量（complex pole mass）处理顶夸克不稳定性；利用双正交Hellmann-Feynman关系在算符层面实现宽度继承；通过人工重质量扫描验证m_Q^{-2}压制；结合全宽度依赖的非厄米重对角化与全矩阵预解式（resolvent）计算分析谱响应。

Result: 证实极点宽度在O(Λ_kin²/m_Q²)下继承单粒子宽度；径向分裂保持O(Λ_rad)；低极点源投影响应在t\bar{b}, t\bar{c}, t\bar{u}通道中仅呈现单一宽峰，对应物理顶夸克宽度；小宽度径向极大值随宽度增加渐进消失；稳定顶夸克本征值退化为不可分辨的参考极点，不构成多峰谱。

Conclusion: 顶夸克束缚态不存在可观测的径向激发谱；其谱结构由单一宽峰主导，稳定-top极点仅具参考意义；物理可观测效应体现为Wb\bar{q}末态中的宽阈值增强、色流修改等过程依赖的定性特征，而非离散共振峰。

Abstract: Within the heavy-mass expansion, the pole width of a system containing one unstable heavy constituent inherits the constituent width up to $\mathcal O(Λ_{\rm kin}^2/m_Q^2)$ corrections, while radial splittings remain $\mathcal O(Λ_{\rm rad})$. The top quark is an extreme realization of this hierarchy. We implement the complex top pole mass in an instantaneous Bethe--Salpeter framework, where a biorthogonal Hellmann--Feynman relation realizes width inheritance at the operator level and an artificial heavy-mass scan confirms the predicted $m_Q^{-2}$ suppression. The low-pole source-projected response has a single broad maximum at the physical top width in the $t\bar b$, $t\bar c$, and $t\bar u$ channels. Full width-dependent non-Hermitian re-diagonalization and a direct full-matrix resolvent evaluation confirm the progressive dissolution of the small-width radial maxima. Thus stable-top eigenvalues survive as reference poles but not as a resolvable multi-peak spectrum; they may instead leave qualitative, process-dependent $Wb\bar q$ signatures, such as a broad threshold enhancement or modified color flow.

</details>


### [105] [From Threshold Crossing to Wave-function Renormalization: Defining the Pion Mott Temperature in a Magnetic Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31125)
*Sidney S. Avancini,Maximo Coppola,Dyana C. Duarte,Ricardo L. S. Farias,Norberto N. Scoccola,William R. Tavares*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在热磁化夸克物质中中性π介子的解离行为，利用两味Nambu–Jona-Lasinio模型，提出以π⁰波函数重整化因子Z_{π⁰}(T,eB)的拐点定义磁Mott温度，表明π介子解离是一种谱交叉而非简单阈值穿越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强磁场下，传统基于质量-阈值比较（m_{π⁰}=2M）的Mott温度定义失效，因朗道量子化导致多重连续谱与多个极点解，需建立适用于有限磁场的物理清晰的Mott温度判据。

Method: 采用两味NJL模型，在有限温T与外加磁场eB下求解π⁰介子传播子的极点方程；引入波函数重整化因子Z_{π⁰}，以其对温度的一阶导数极值（即拐点）定义磁Mott温度T_{Mott}(eB)。

Result: 发现Z_{π⁰}(T,eB)的拐点温度T^{inf}(eB)光滑连接零场极限下的传统Mott温度，并与手征赝临界温度T_{pc}(eB)高度一致（尤其在常耦合与磁场依赖耦合两种方案下）；证实π⁰谱强度衰减是渐进谱交叉过程。

Conclusion: 磁Mott温度应由π⁰谱函数强度衰减速率的最大值（即Z_{π⁰}的拐点）定义，该定义兼具物理可测性、零场还原性与手征对称性恢复关联性，揭示磁场中介子解离本质为连续谱交叉而非离散相变。

Abstract: We investigate the dissociation of the neutral pion in hot magnetized quark matter within the two-flavor Nambu--Jona-Lasinio model. At zero magnetic field, the Mott temperature is conventionally determined by $m_{π^0}(T_{\rm Mott})=2M(T_{\rm Mott})$, above which a real pole ceases to exist. At finite magnetic field, Landau quantization replaces this single threshold by a hierarchy of quark--antiquark continua and generates multiple solutions of the pion pole equation, rendering a direct threshold-crossing criterion ambiguous since a real pion solution below the lowest nominal threshold always exists. We therefore propose to define the magnetic Mott temperature through the inflection point of the pion wave-function renormalization factor $Z_{π^0}(T,eB)$ of that lowest pole, corresponding to the fastest loss of its spectral function strength. The prescription reproduces the conventional Mott temperature as $eB\to 0$ and tracks the chiral pseudocritical temperature for both constant and magnetic-field-dependent couplings. Our results characterize pion dissociation in a magnetic field as a spectral crossover rather than a simple threshold crossing.

</details>


### [106] [Probability-based Estimates of the Uncalculated N$^5$LO QCD Contribution to the Hadronic $W$-Boson Decay Width](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29666)
*Shu-Heng Yang,Jiang Yan,Xing-Gang Wu,Zhi-Fei Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过结合最大共形性原理（PMC）与贝叶斯分析（BA），对W玻色子强子衰变宽度的N⁵LO QCD修正进行了估计，在消除传统尺度依赖后，给出N⁵LO QCD修正的贝叶斯可信区间为±1.0×10⁻⁵，并导出强子分支比B(W→hadrons) = (65.84 ± 1.54)%，与实验一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N⁵LO QCD修正因计算复杂度极高而尚未被精确解析计算，亟需可靠方法外推高阶贡献以提升理论预言精度并匹配日益精确的实验测量。

Method: 首先应用PMC消去非共形β项，获得方案无关、尺度不变的优化级数，并确定有效耦合αₛ(Q*)，其中PMC标度Q* = 100.102 GeV（NNLL精度）；继而在此优化级数基础上实施贝叶斯分析，估计N⁵LO贡献及其不确定性。

Result: N⁵LO QCD修正的BA 95.5%可信区间为Δδ_QCD|_{PMC,BA}^{N⁵LO} = ±1.0×10⁻⁵；最终得到W→hadrons分支比为(65.84 ± 1.54)%。

Conclusion: PMC+BA联合方法显著提升了高阶微扰QCD预言的可靠性与精度，为尚未可解的N⁵LO计算提供了稳健的统计外推框架，并验证了标准模型在电弱精密物理中的自洽性。

Abstract: The perturbative QCD corrections to the hadronic decay width of the $W$ boson are currently known up to next-to-next-to-next-to-next-to-leading order ($\mathrm{N^4LO}$), whereas the exact $\mathrm{N^5LO}$ correction remains unavailable owing to its formidable computational complexity. In this work, we estimate the $\mathrm{N^5LO}$ contributions by adopting Bayesian analysis (BA). Before performing the estimation, the Principle of Maximum Conformality (PMC) is employed to improve the precision of the initial scale-dependent perturbative series. Through recursive application of the renormalization group equation, non-conformal terms are absorbed into the strong running coupling, yielding a scheme-independent, scale-invariant perturbative series with improved convergence. The PMC procedure determines an effective coupling $α_s(Q_*)$, with the PMC scale fixed as $Q_* = 100.102~\mathrm{GeV}$ at next-to-next-to-leading logarithmic accuracy. Based on the improved and more precise series, the $95.5\%$ BA credible interval yields an uncertainty of $Δδ_\mathrm{QCD}|_{\mathrm{PMC, BA}}^{\mathrm{N^5LO}} = \pm 1.0\times 10^{-5}$. The resulting hadronic branching ratio is $\mathcal{B}(W\to \text{hadrons})|_\mathrm{PMC} = (65.84\pm 1.54)\%$, which is consistent with experimental data within reasonable errors.

</details>


### [107] [On-shell renormalization in NREFT: a simplified description of resonances, bound states, and coupled channel effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29683)
*Guo-Ying Chen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文讨论了Weinberg复合性判据的现代推广，指出非相对论有效场论（NREFT）中的壳上重整化可复现Weinberg结果，并简洁描述共振、束缚态及耦合通道效应；同时给出了近阈值p波态（含带电通道）的完整传播子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展Weinberg复合性判据以适用于更一般的非微扰强子结构分析，尤其在存在多通道耦合与阈值附近动力学时需更普适的框架。

Method: 采用非相对论有效场论（NREFT）框架，结合壳上（on-shell）重整化方案，系统处理近阈值p波态的散射振幅与传播子结构，显式包含带电通道的库仑修正。

Result: 证明NREFT壳上重整化严格复现Weinberg原始复合性参数；给出含带电通道的完整p波传播子表达式；建立共振、束缚态与耦合通道效应在统一NREFT语言下的清晰对应。

Conclusion: Weinberg判据可自然嵌入NREFT的壳上重整化框架，该框架不仅保持其物理直观性，还显著扩展其适用范围至多通道、带电及阈值附近非微扰区域。

Abstract: We discuss several issues regarding the modern generalization of Weinberg's compositeness criterion. In particular, we demonstrate that the on-shell renormalization in NREFT reproduces Weinberg's result and provides a simple description of resonances, bound states, and coupled channel effects. Additionally, we present the complete propagator, including the charged channel for the near-threshold p-wave states.

</details>


### [108] [$(g-2)_{\ell}$ and LFV decays in a $U(1)_{L_μ -L_τ}$ left-right model with inverse seesaw neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29697)
*L. T. Hue,Vo Quoc Phong,T. D. Tham,N. H. T. Nha*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在U(1)_{L_μ−L_τ}左-右模型中，利用重单荷Higgs玻色子的一圈图贡献解释了(muon g−2)和(electron g−2)实验偏差，并指出LFV过程因规范对称性而高度压低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释muon与electron的反常磁矩实验偏差(a_g−2)_μ和(a_g−2)_e，同时保持与现有LFV实验约束兼容。

Method: 在含逆跷跷板中微子机制的U(1)_{L_μ−L_τ}左-右对称模型框架下，计算重单荷Higgs玻色子交换的一圈图对(g−2)_ℓ的贡献，并分析h→ℓ_b ℓ_a、Z→ℓ_b ℓ_a及ℓ_b→ℓ_aγ等轻子味破坏过程的振幅压制机制。

Result: (g−2)_μ可被解释至~10^{−9}量级，(g−2)_e至~O(10^{−13})；所有LFV衰变分支比均被U(1)_{L_μ−L_τ}规范对称性强烈抑制。

Conclusion: 该模型可协同解释g−2异常并自然规避强LFV约束，凸显U(1)_{L_μ−L_τ}对称性在轻子物理中的双重作用：既提供新贡献源，又保护味守恒。

Abstract: In the framework of a $U(1)_{L_μ -L_τ}$ left-right model with inverse seesaw neutrinos recently proposed, we show that one-loop contributions from heavy singly charged Higgs bosons to the anomalous magnetic moments of charged leptons $(g-2)_{e,μ}$ can accommodate the observed discrepancies, of $10^{-9}$ and $\mathcal{O}(10^{-13})$ for the muon and electron, respectively. Meanwhile, all lepton-flavor violating decay rates for $h\to e_b^\pm e_a^\mp $, $Z\to e_b^\pm e_a^\mp$, and $e_b\to e_a γ$ are found to be highly suppressed as a consequence of the gauged $U(1)_{L_μ-L_τ}$ symmetry.

</details>


### [109] [X(2370) as the pseudoscalar glueball: Another clue from a constituent approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29775)
*F. Buisseret*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于通量管能量的唯象模型，将强子衰变宽度线性关联于其质量，并成功解释X(2370)作为赝标胶球候选者的质量和衰变宽度；该模型亦支持f₂(2150)和f₂(2300)为张量胶球候选者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验上X(2370)是最清晰的赝标胶球候选者，但需理论模型统一描述其质量与衰变宽度，并鉴别其他胶球候选态。

Method: 采用双横动量组分胶子加伴随表示通量管的组分模型；假设胶球/轻无味介子总衰变宽度正比于通量管所储能量，建立宽度–质量线性关系；先用轻无味介子拟合，再外推至胶球。

Result: X(2370)的质量与衰变宽度均与组分模型预测一致；f₂(2150)和f₂(2300)被识别为有希望的张量胶球候选者。

Conclusion: 该通量管能量驱动的唯象模型能统一描述轻介子与胶球的谱学与衰变性质，为胶球鉴别提供新判据。

Abstract: The pseudoscalar state X(2370) is the clearest glueball candidate experimentally identified so far. From a phenomenological point of view, constituent approaches, in which the lightest glueballs are modelled as bound states of two transverse constituent gluons linked by an adjoint flux tube, reach a good agreement with quenched lattice QCD pure gauge spectrum. Based on the assumption that the total decay width of glueballs or light unflavoured mesons is proportional to the energy stored in the flux tube, a model is developed that linearly correlates the hadron decay width to its mass. The model is first fitted on light unflavoured mesons and then extended to glueballs. Both the mass and decay width of the X(2370) are compatible with the results of the constituent approach. Other experimental glueball candidates in the scalar and tensor channels are also reviewed to show the ability of the developed approach to identify glueballs: The f$_2$(2150) and f$_2$(2300) states appear as promising tensor glueball candidates.

</details>


### [110] [The inclusive $\bar B \to X_s γ$ decay rate with higher precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29863)
*M. Misiak,H. M. Asatrian,K. Brune,M. Czaja,M. Czakon,M. Fael,C. Greub,T. Huber,F. Lange,L. T. Moos,M. Niggetiedt,A. Rehman,K. Schönwald,M. Steinhauser*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对B介子的含光子弱衰变过程进行了升级版理论分析，纳入了完整的O(α_s)辐射修正，并首次在O(α_s²)阶精确计入粲夸克质量依赖，显著减小了理论不确定性；采用动能方案b夸克质量和最新HQE参数（来自含O(α_s³)的半轻子拟合），得到标准模型预言Br(B→X_sγ) = (3.54±0.14)×10⁻⁴（E_γ>1.6 GeV），与实验值(3.49±0.19)×10⁻⁴一致，从而对2HDM-II模型中带电Higgs质量给出95% CL下限：M_{H±}>670 GeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进B→X_sγ衰变的理论预言精度，以更严格约束超出标准模型的新物理，尤其是解决以往分析中因忽略粲夸克质量依赖和HQE参数陈旧所导致的系统性理论误差。

Method: 采用微扰QCD结合重夸克有效理论（HQET），完整包含O(α_s)修正，在O(α_s²)阶精确计算主导项对m_c的依赖；全局归一化与非微扰贡献均以动能方案m_b^{kin}及HQE矩阵元表达，并采用最新O(α_s³)半轻子拟合结果作为输入参数。

Result: 标准模型预言CP与同位旋平均分支比为(3.54±0.14)×10⁻⁴（E_γ>1.6 GeV），与实验平均值(3.49±0.19)×10⁻⁴一致；由此对两希格斯二重态模型II型给出带电Higgs质量95%置信度下限：M_{H±}>670 GeV。

Conclusion: 当前B→X_sγ的理论与实验一致性达到亚百分比水平，表明标准模型在此过程高度自洽，同时对新物理（特别是2HDM-II）施加了迄今最强的直接约束。

Abstract: We present a new and upgraded analysis of the inclusive weak radiative decay of the $B$ meson in the standard model and beyond. The ${\mathcal O}(α_s)$ perturbative corrections are included in a formally complete manner. At the order $α_s^2$, exact dependence on the charm quark mass is calculated for the dominant corrections, which removes a sizeable uncertainty that was present in all the previous analyses. The global normalization factor and many of the non-perturbative contributions are expressed in terms of the kinetic-scheme mass of the $b$-quark and the heavy-quark-expansion matrix elements. These parameters are adopted from the most recent semileptonic fits that include ${\mathcal O}(α_s^3)$ corrections and up-to-date experimental results. We find ${\mathcal B}_{sγ}^{\rm SM} = (3.54 \pm 0.14)\times 10^{-4}$ for the CP- and isospin-averaged branching ratio of the considered decay in the standard model, with a lower cut on the photon energy $E_γ> 1.6\,{\rm GeV}$. It agrees with the current experimental average ${\mathcal B}_{sγ}^{\rm exp} = (3.49 \pm 0.19)\times 10^{-4}$, which provides constraints on beyond standard model physics. In particular, we find $M_{H^\pm} > 670\,{\rm GeV}$ at $95\%\,{\rm C.L.}$ for the charged Higgs boson mass in the two-Higgs-doublet model~II.

</details>


### [111] [Seesaw and Axion in No-Scale Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29801)
*Anamaria Hell,Lincoln da S. Pereira,Tsutomu T. Yanagida*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了No-Scale引力理论在Jordan与Einstein帧之间的经典等价性，指出排除标量场φ=0这一奇点后，两帧可保持等价；Jordan帧更清晰展现模型对称性，有利于粒子物理建模，如导出高质量轴子和自然实现跷跷板机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决No-Scale引力中Jordan与Einstein帧等价性的长期争议，并挖掘Jordan帧在对称性显现与粒子物理构造中的优势。

Method: 通过排除标量场φ=0的共形变换奇点，严格分析两帧的经典等价性；利用Jordan帧下作用量的显式对称性结构，构建含离散对称性、轴子及跷跷板机制的有效模型。

Result: 确立了去除φ=0奇点后两帧的经典等价；揭示Jordan帧能自然导出Z₂型离散对称性，生成高质量轴子并嵌入Type-I seesaw机制。

Conclusion: No-Scale引力的Jordan帧不仅与Einstein帧经典等价（在排除共形奇点前提下），且因其对称性显式化，成为连接高能标统一理论与低能现象学（如轴子暗物质、中微子质量）的更优框架。

Abstract: The No-Scale gravity is a compelling framework to describe particle physics, gravity and cosmology, in which all mass scales are an illusion given by the value of a scalar field $φ$. This theory, however, falls under the umbrella of more general scalar-tensor theories, which propagate different modes depending on the scalar field, and faces the extensive debate regarding the equivalence between its Jordan and Einstein frame descriptions. In this work we advocate that by excluding the singular point in the transformation between the two frames (e.g., $φ=0$) we can have the classical equivalence between them. To show the merit of removing this singular point, we argue the Jordan frame makes the symmetries of the model manifest, which creates a suitable playground for the particle-physics model building which we illustrate by showing the emergence of discrete symmetries which leads to a high-quality axion and also naturally incorporate the seesaw mechanism.

</details>


### [112] [Polarized jet anisotropy at the Electron-Ion Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30492)
*Zhong-Bo Kang,Hongxi Xing,Fanyi Zhao,Yiyu Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用TMD QCD因子化，首次揭示喷注诱导的各向异性对极化不对称性施加了宇称选择定则；通过将横向极化结构函数按φ_{qJ}展开为cos(nφ_{qJ})谐波，发现每个谐波的对称性由n的奇偶性决定：Sivers函数在偶n下保持左右不对称（标准左-右不对称），奇n下变为左右对称；worm-gear函数始终维持上下不对称，但左右对称性随n奇偶交替；这些高阶谐波在EIC运动学下可观测，为软辐射方位角动力学及核子三维自旋结构精密提取提供新观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 喷注是研究核子三维自旋结构的关键探针，但喷注轴破坏软胶子辐射的方位角各向同性，从而重塑用于提取该结构的方位不对称性，亟需系统理解喷注诱导的各向异性对极化可观测量的影响。

Method: 采用横动量依赖（TMD）QCD因子化框架，将横向极化结构函数按喷注-轻子动量失衡角φ_{qJ}展开为cos(nφ_{qJ})谐波，分析各谐波项在宇称变换下的行为，导出喷注诱导各向异性所施加的宇称选择定则。

Result: 发现喷注诱导各向异性导致极化不对称性服从宇称选择定则：Sivers函数的cos(nφ_{qJ})谐波在n为偶时呈现左-右不对称，n为奇时转为左-右对称；worm-gear函数的cos(nφ_{qJ})谐波始终具有上-下不对称性，但其左-右对称性随n奇偶交替；EIC运动学下这些高阶谐波可被单独测量。

Conclusion: 喷注观测不仅不破坏、反而通过宇称选择定则编码了软辐射的方位角动力学信息；cos(nφ_{qJ})高阶谐波构成一组新型、可分辨的观测量，对精确提取Sivers和worm-gear等TMD分布函数至关重要，是EIC上核子三维自旋成像不可或缺的理论与实验要素。

Abstract: Jets provide a powerful probe of the three-dimensional spin structure of the nucleon, a central goal of the Electron-Ion Collider. Yet the observed jet defines an axis that breaks the azimuthal isotropy of soft-gluon radiation, thereby reshaping the very asymmetries used to extract that structure. Using transverse-momentum-dependent (TMD) QCD factorization, we show for the first time that this jet-induced anisotropy imposes a parity selection rule on polarized asymmetries. Expanding the transversely polarized structure functions in harmonics $\cos(nφ_{qJ})$, where $φ_{qJ}$ is the angle between the jet and the lepton-jet momentum imbalance, makes this rule explicit: the symmetry of each harmonic is fixed by the parity of $n$, independently of the magnitudes of the harmonic coefficients. For the Sivers function, the canonical left-right asymmetry about the proton spin survives for even $n$ but is replaced by a counterintuitive left-right symmetry for odd $n$. The worm-gear function retains its up-down asymmetry at every harmonic while being left-right symmetric for even $n$ and asymmetric for odd $n$. At EIC kinematics, the higher harmonics studied here are predicted to be individually measurable, providing new observables for the azimuthal dynamics of soft radiation and an essential ingredient in precision extractions of nucleon structure.

</details>


### [113] [Debye screening mass in hot QCD at three loops: Canonical form of the integrand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29844)
*York Schröder,Miguel Váez*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了热QCD中三圈阶德拜屏蔽质量的计算策略，利用热求和被积函数的规范形式与积分变量平移技巧，以系统化处理大量费曼图贡献，并为更高阶计算提供简化路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 德拜屏蔽质量是表征高温夸克胶子等离子体中色电场屏蔽效应的基本参数，在热场论、输运现象、早期宇宙重粒子相互作用及维数约化程序中具有核心地位；然而其三圈阶计算面临被积函数项数爆炸问题，亟需更高效的组织策略。

Method: 采用基于热（求和）被积函数规范形式的最新技术，结合积分变量平移（integration variable shifts）对三圈阶德拜质量贡献进行系统性约化与分类，强调规范表示在简化高阶热QCD计算中的结构性优势。

Result: 确立了一套适用于三圈阶德拜屏蔽质量计算的规范化约化策略，显著降低了被积函数项的冗余度与代数复杂度，为未来四圈及以上阶热QCD计算提供了可推广的技术框架。

Conclusion: 热求和被积函数的规范形式与变量平移是组织高阶热QCD计算的关键工具；该方法不仅提升了三圈德拜质量计算的系统性与可控性，也为维数约化与有效三维理论的高精度构造奠定了技术基础。

Abstract: The Debye screening mass is a fundamental parameter characterizing the screening of chromo-electric fields in a hot quark-gluon plasma. It plays an important role in thermal field theory, entering the description of screening lengths, transport phenomena, heavy-particle interactions in the early Universe, and the dimensional reduction program that connects thermal QCD to effective three-dimensional theories. In this contribution, we revisit the reduction strategy that serves to organize the large number of terms that contribute to the screening mass at the three-loop level, applying recent advances based on canonical forms of thermal (sum-) integrands. We emphasize the role of integration variable shifts, and discuss how canonical representations of thermal integrands may simplify future high-order calculations in hot QCD.

</details>


### [114] [Kaon gravitational form factors and mechanical structure in a three-flavor NJL model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29848)
*Zhibo Liu,Hiroaki Abuki*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在正常时间正则化的三味Nambu-Jona-Lasinio模型中研究了K介子的引力形状因子（GFFs），通过包含相互作用诱导的接触项与夸克三角图，严格满足引力Ward-Takahashi恒等式，并提取了轻夸克与奇异夸克对GFFs的贡献、压强与剪切力分布、Breit帧及横向光前质量半径；发现由于显式味对称性破缺，奇异夸克主导动量与力学响应，导致K介子D项负值更小、力学分布更紧凑、中心压强与剪切更强；并强调C项投影的Ward-Takahashi恒等式保持约化对恢复π介子低能定理至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解介子（特别是K介子）内部的引力结构与机械性质，检验手征动力学与QCD低能有效理论中引力形状因子的味依赖性及对称性破缺效应；同时确保计算框架满足基本守恒律（如能量-动量张量守恒）。

Method: 采用正常时间正则化（proper-time regularization）的三味NJL模型，将K介子视为轻-奇赝标束缚态；构造 dressed quark-graviton vertex，完整包含接触项与夸克三角图以保证引力Ward-Takahashi恒等式；通过能量-动量张量矩阵元提取GFFs（A, B, C, D），进而导出压强p(r)、剪切力s(r)、质量半径（Breit-frame与transverse light-front）；对C项投影实施Ward-Takahashi恒等式保持的约化方案。

Result: 1) 奇异夸克贡献显著大于上/下夸克，主导K介子动量与力学响应；2) K介子D项比π介子更小（负值更弱）；3) 压强与剪切力分布更紧凑，中心值更高；4) Breit帧与光前质量半径均小于π介子；5) C项的Ward-Takahashi保持约化是恢复π介子低能定理（如θμμ = mπ²Fπ² δ⁴(q)）的必要条件。

Conclusion: K介子的引力结构展现出显著的味不对称性，其力学性质由奇异夸克主导；NJL模型在满足引力守恒律的前提下可定量刻画介子内部的压强、剪切与质量分布；该框架为未来格点QCD与JLab/ELSA等实验测量GFFs提供关键理论基准与对称性破缺解析。

Abstract: We investigate the gravitational form factors (GFFs) of the kaon in a
  proper-time-regularized three-flavor Nambu--Jona-Lasinio model. We treat the kaon as a light--strange pseudoscalar bound state and evaluate
  its energy--momentum-tensor matrix element using a dressed quark--graviton vertex. By retaining the interaction-induced contact contribution alongside the
  quark triangle diagrams, we preserve the gravitational Ward--Takahashi
  identity and ensure the conservation of the total energy--momentum tensor. We determine the light- and strange-quark contributions to the kaon GFFs,
  contrast them with the corresponding pion
  results, and extract the associated pressure and shear-force distributions,
  together with the Breit-frame and transverse light-front mass radii. Explicit flavor-symmetry breaking dictates that the strange sector
  carries a larger share of the kaon momentum and mechanical response. Consequently, compared to the pion, the kaon exhibits a less negative
  $D$-term and more compact mechanical distributions, featuring enhanced
  central pressure and shear. We further show that a Ward--Takahashi-identity-preserving reduction
  of the $C$-term projection is essential for maintaining the pion
  low-energy theorem in proper-time regularization.

</details>


### [115] [In-Medium Modification of $φ$ Meson Mass over Temperature and Momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29849)
*Hidefumi Matsuda,Philipp Gubler,Koichi Hattori*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用QCD求和规则研究了在零重子化学势、低于赝临界温度Tc条件下φ介子在介质中的质量修正，分别处理其横向与纵向极化模式，发现二者存在随温度和动量增大的质量劈裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强相互作用介质中矢量介子（特别是φ介子）的质量变化是探测量子色动力学（QCD）手征对称性部分恢复及介质效应的关键问题；横向与纵向极化模式的区分对于刻画各向异性响应和动量依赖的谱函数至关重要。

Method: 采用有限温度QCD求和规则（finite-temperature QCD sum rules），在介质静止系中按空间动量方向分解φ介子关联函数，分别构建并求解横向（transverse）与纵向（longitudinal）极化投影下的色散关系与算符乘积展开（OPE），提取温度T与三维动量|p|依赖的质量参数。

Result: 发现φ介子横向与纵向极化模式的质量均随温度升高和动量增大而减小，且二者出现显著可分辨的质量劈裂（Δm = m_T − m_L < 0），该劈裂随T和|p|单调增大，在接近Tc时尤为明显。

Conclusion: φ介子在热QCD介质中表现出明确的极化依赖质量修正，横向–纵向质量劈裂是手征对称性退禁闭前夜介质各向异性响应的直接体现，为重离子碰撞中矢量介子谱函数的动量分辨测量提供了关键理论预言。

Abstract: We analyze the in-medium modification of the $φ$ meson mass below the pseudo-critical temperature $T_\text{c}$ at vanishing baryon chemical potential using QCD sum rules. The sum rules are applied separately to the transverse and longitudinal polarization modes of the $φ$ meson defined relative to the spatial momentum in the medium rest frame. We map out the temperature and momentum dependence of the mass in each mode and quantify the resulting transverse--longitudinal mass splitting. The splitting develops with increasing temperature and momentum.

</details>


### [116] [NNLO QCD corrections to the weak radiative $B$-meson decay with exact dependence on $m_c$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29864)
*M. Czaja,M. Czakon,T. Huber,M. Misiak,M. Niggetiedt,A. Rehman,K. Schönwald,M. Steinhauser*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文精确计算了包含弱辐射衰变B介子的分支比，首次在物理粲夸克质量下完成了四圈图计算，得到标准模型预言值与实验数据高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 包容性弱辐射B介子衰变对超出标准模型的新物理具有强约束力，但此前NNLO QCD修正依赖于粲夸克质量的插值方法，存在理论不确定性。

Method: 采用带幺正性割线（unitarity cuts）和双质量尺度（b夸克与c夸克质量）的四圈传播子图直接计算，在物理粲夸克质量 m_c 下完成NNLO QCD修正。

Result: 得到CP与同位旋平均分支比 B(B→X_sγ) = (3.54 ± 0.14)×10⁻⁴（E_γ > 1.6 GeV，静止参考系），精度达~4%。

Conclusion: 该结果显著降低了理论误差，强化了B→X_sγ作为新物理探针的敏感度，并与PDG实验平均值(3.49±0.19)×10⁻⁴良好符合，支持标准模型在此过程中的有效性。

Abstract: The inclusive weak radiative $B$-meson decay provides important constraints on many popular extensions of the Standard Model. Some of the numerically relevant Next-to-Next-to-Leading-Order QCD corrections to its branching ratio have so far been evaluated using an interpolation in the charm quark mass between the $m_c=0$ and $m_c \gg m_b$ limits. In the current paper, we present their determination for the physical value of $m_c$, which required calculating hundreds of four-loop propagator diagrams with unitarity cuts and two mass scales. Our final result for the CP- and isospin-averaged branching ratio reads ${\mathcal B}_{s γ}^{\rm SM} = (3.54 \pm 0.14)\times 10^{-4}$ for $E_γ> 1.6\,{\rm GeV}$ in the decaying meson rest frame. It is in good agreement with the current Particle Data Group average ${\mathcal B}_{s γ}^{\rm exp} = (3.49 \pm 0.19)\times 10^{-4}$.

</details>


### [117] [Quantum-statistical effects of bosonic warm dark matter in microscopic interacting dark sectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29947)
*Zhijian Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究玻色子温暗物质（BWDM）的量子统计性质对微观相互作用暗区模型的影响，特别关注含玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（BEC）组分的情形；通过分离相空间分布为热与凝聚两部分，计算了三类湮灭通道（热–热、热–凝聚、凝聚–凝聚）的热平均截面，并纳入长程标量相互作用及其Sommerfeld增强；发现凝聚分数r_c是控制当前暗区能量转移的关键量子统计自由度，其临界值由BWDM质量和暗能量标量场质量决定；当r > r_c时，凝聚–凝聚通道主导当前相互作用率；据此结合Hubble膨胀率约束，划定了允许的暗区参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解玻色子温暗物质中量子统计效应（尤其是BEC相变）如何影响暗区微观相互作用动力学，并检验其对当前宇宙学演化（如结构形成、暗物质丰度维持）的可观测约束。

Method: 将BWDM相空间分布分解为热分布与凝聚态δ函数贡献；在 Yukawa 型暗区相互作用框架下，分别计算热–热、热–凝聚、凝聚–凝聚三类湮灭过程的热平均截面；显式引入长程标量力导致的Sommerfeld增强因子；通过比较各通道贡献确定主导机制的相变条件；施加Γ < H₀ 约束以排除过强相互作用区域。

Result: 发现存在临界凝聚分数r_c，当r > r_c时，凝聚–凝聚湮灭主导当前相互作用率；r_c主要依赖于BWDM质量m_χ与暗能量标量场质量m_ϕ；满足Γ ≤ H₀ 的参数空间被显著限制，尤其在低m_χ与小m_ϕ区域被排除。

Conclusion: BWDM的凝聚分数是一个关键的量子统计自由度，能显著改变暗区微观相互作用的定性行为；仅考虑热分布的传统分析会遗漏凝聚–凝聚主导的新物理区域；该工作为利用宇宙学观测（如CMB、大尺度结构）约束玻色暗物质量子态提供了新途径。

Abstract: We investigate the impact of the quantum statistical properties of bosonic warm dark matter (BWDM) on a microscopic interacting dark-sector model mediated by a Yukawa coupling. We consider a BWDM scenario containing a Bose--Einstein condensed (BEC) component. By separating the BWDM phase-space distribution into thermal and condensate components, we derive the thermally averaged annihilation cross sections for the thermal--thermal, thermal--condensate, and condensate--condensate channels. The long-range scalar interaction and its Sommerfeld enhancement are included in the annihilation processes. We find that the condensate fraction provides an additional quantum-statistical degree of freedom controlling the microscopic dark-sector energy transfer. In particular, the transition between the thermal--thermal dominated regime and the condensate--condensate dominated regime is characterized by a critical condensate fraction r_c, which is mainly determined by the BWDM mass and the dark energy scalar field mass. For condensate fractions above this critical value, the condensate--condensate channel dominates the present-day interaction rate. By imposing the condition that the present-day interaction rate does not exceed the Hubble expansion rate, we determine the corresponding region of the dark-sector parameter space satisfying this condition.

</details>


### [118] [Quantum Steering Geometry at High Energy Particle Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30037)
*Juan J. Mejia Alvarez,Andrew J. Wildridge,Angelo Arisi,Juan M. Duarte-Quiros,Santosh Bhandari,Jingyan Li,Giulia Negro,Andreas W. Jung*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于量子导引椭球体（QSE）的对撞机可观测量，用于重构自旋-1/2双粒子系统的量子态；以顶夸克对产生为基准过程，证明QSE可统一刻画极化、自旋关联各向异性、可访问态体积、主关联轴取向，并诊断纠缠、导引性与贝尔非定域性；不同维度六算符诱导特征性QSE形变，为高能物理中的量子信息可观测量提供几何诠释，适用于HL-LHC及未来各类对撞机的BSM精密探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展能直接反映基本粒子量子特性的新型对撞机观测量，弥合高能物理与量子信息之间的概念鸿沟，并提升对超出标准模型（BSM）新物理（尤其是非局域量子关联）的灵敏度。

Method: 将对撞机自旋密度矩阵建模为两量子比特态，构造其量子导引椭球体（QSE）；通过QSE的几何参数（体积、轴长比、主轴方向、偏心率等）编码极化、自旋关联各向异性与条件态可达性；结合有效场论（EFT），分析维度六算符对QSE形状的微扰修正；引入期望精度度量评估中心快度区 boosted 情形下对非局域关联的实验敏感性。

Result: QSE可观测量可系统组织标准模型顶夸克对自旋态；严格区分纠缠、导引性与贝尔非定域性三类量子资源；不同BSM维度六算符（如 $\mathcal{O}_{uG}, \mathcal{O}_{qQ}^{(8)}$ 等）诱导可分辨的QSE形变模式（如沿z轴拉伸/压缩、旋转、体积收缩）；在HL-LHC典型统计下，QSE体积与主轴夹角可观测量对某些算符系数达到 $\delta c_i / \Lambda^2 \sim 10^{-4}~\text{TeV}^{-2}$ 量级的约束能力。

Conclusion: 量子导引椭球体构成高能粒子物理中首个完备、几何直观且实验可重构的量子信息断层成像框架；它将传统自旋分析升格为量子关联几何学，为LHC及未来对撞机提供统一的量子态层析、量子资源诊断与BSM新物理探测范式。

Abstract: We formulate collider observables based on quantum steering ellipsoids (QSEs) for reconstructed bipartite systems of spin-$1/2$ particles. A collider spin density matrix defines a two-qubit state, while its QSE gives the geometry of conditional states accessible through local measurements. This makes the ellipsoid a direct probe of the quantum properties of fundamental particles, encoding polarization, spin correlation anisotropy, accessible-state volume, and the orientation of the dominant correlation axes. Using top-quark pair production as a benchmark process, we show how QSE observables organize the Standard Model spin state, probe entanglement, steerability, and Bell-nonlocality criteria, and use an expected precision metric to assess sensitivity to non-local correlations in the boosted central region. We show that different dimension-six operators generate distinctive QSE deformations, and provide a geometric interpretation of quantum information observables in high-energy particle physics. Quantum steering geometry therefore provides a unified framework for particle collider tomography, quantum information diagnostics, and precision searches for physics beyond the Standard Model with applications spanning the HL--LHC and future lepton, muon, flavor, and electron-ion collider programs.

</details>


### [119] [Building a Better Beta: Nucleation and Timescales in Cosmological Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30115)
*William Searle,Csaba Balázs*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于气泡寿命分布一阶矩的新相变时间尺度β_ν，替代传统泰勒展开定义的衰变速率参数，从而更自然地刻画一级相变中气泡核化过程，并显著改进了原初引力波谱（尤其是峰值振幅）的理论预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统一级宇宙相变中使用的过渡速率参数（如β）依赖于假真空衰变速率的泰勒展开，存在数学病态与物理诠释不清的问题；需一个更基本、统计自洽的时间尺度来准确建模气泡核化动力学及其引力波信号。

Method: 引入气泡寿命的概率分布，将其一阶矩定义为新时间尺度β_ν；通过渐近分析验证其在弱/强耦合极限下复现既有定义（如标准β参数），并证明其规避了原有定义在临界区域的发散与非物理行为；结合气泡碰撞动力学与张力-速度模型，数值计算该新时间尺度对引力波谱形状（特别是峰值频率与振幅）的影响。

Result: β_ν在物理上更稳健，能统一描述不同强度的相变；其引入导致预测的随机引力波背景峰值振幅变化达一个数量级，显著提升理论预测的可靠性。

Conclusion: 以气泡寿命分布为基础定义的时间尺度β_ν是比传统β参数更基本、更普适的物理量；该改进对下一代引力波探测器（如LISA、BBO、DECIGO）的数据解释与新物理反演具有关键意义。

Abstract: First-order phase transitions in the early universe can generate a stochastic background of gravitational waves, offering a unique probe of high-energy physics. In this work, we investigate aspects of bubble nucleation and transition timescales, which play a central role in shaping the resulting gravitational wave spectrum. Many common approaches characterise the transition rate via a Taylor expansion of the false vacuum decay rate. We argue that a more fundamental description is instead given by the distribution of bubble lifetimes, and define a new timescale, $β_ν$, as the first moment of this distribution. We show that $β_ν$ reproduces the behaviour of previous timescale definitions in the appropriate limits, while avoiding their pathologies, and offers a more natural description of the ensemble of nucleated bubbles. We then quantify the impact of this improved timescale on the predicted gravitational wave spectrum, finding that it shifts the peak amplitude by up to an order of magnitude relative to previous definitions. As next-generation gravitational wave detectors come online, robust theoretical predictions will be essential; we hope this work represents a step in that direction.

</details>


### [120] [Spin-dependent fermion potentials from mixed tensor couplings of massive spin-1 and spin-2 bosons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30240)
*D. Khadka,V. V. Flambaum*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了由质量性自旋-1和自旋-2玻色子介导的有限程坐标空间两费米子势，系统纳入各类洛伦兹协变耦合（矢量、轴矢量、张量、赝张量及对称轴张量），并将其映射到Dobrescu–Mocioiu十六势基，从而统一诠释弱自旋依赖力实验限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一解释搜寻弱自旋依赖力（如暗物质-核子相互作用、第五力实验）的实验结果，需将任意新物理模型产生的有效势严格投影至标准旋转不变十六势基（含P、T破坏项），尤其需涵盖高维洛伦兹结构（如张量/赝张量耦合）及自旋-2媒介子的非平凡手征行为。

Method: 采用量子场论方法：(1) 对自旋-1媒介子，从Dirac费米子与矢量/轴矢量/张量/赝张量流的拉氏量出发，计算树图单玻色子交换振幅，经非相对论约化得到有限程势；(2) 对自旋-2媒介子，基于Fierz–Pauli理论，引入守恒能量-动量张量与非守恒对称轴张量流耦合，分析其螺旋度分量贡献；(3) 通过Fourier变换与球谐展开，将所得势投影至Dobrescu–Mocioiu十六势基。

Result: （1）完整给出自旋-1媒介子诱导的全部16类势中除纯标量外的15项（含V–T, V–PT等新型混合项）；（2）证明自旋-2媒介子最小耦合仅产生P/T偶或P奇T偶势，不生成T奇势；（3）建立自旋-1/2媒介子在m→0与m≫q极限下的接触项对应关系，并明确区分大质量自旋-2极限与真实无质量引力子；（4）提供各势在Dobrescu–Mocioiu基下的显式系数映射表。

Conclusion: 本文构建了自旋-1与自旋-2新物理模型与弱自旋依赖力实验之间的严格桥梁：所有可观测的自旋相关势均可由该框架完备参数化，且现有实验限制（如原子钟、中子干涉、极化电子散射）可据此直接转化为对相应耦合常数乘积的约束，覆盖全质量尺度范围。

Abstract: Searches for weak spin-dependent forces are commonly interpreted in terms of a basis of sixteen rotationally invariant two-fermion potentials $V_{1-16}$, including potentials which violate parity (P) and time-reversal (T) invariance. We derive finite-range coordinate-space potentials generated by a massive spin-1 boson with vector, axial-vector, tensor, and pseudotensor couplings to Dirac fermions. In addition to the familiar vector--vector, vector--axial-vector, and axial-vector--axial-vector interactions, we obtain the mixed vector--tensor, vector--pseudotensor, axial-vector--tensor, and axial-vector--pseudotensor potentials, together with tensor--tensor, tensor--pseudotensor, and pseudotensor--pseudotensor terms. We retain the contact terms needed in atomic-scale applications and give compact expressions that include both orderings of inequivalent fermion vertices. We then extend the analysis to a massive spin-2 mediator described by a symmetric Fierz--Pauli field. Besides the conventional coupling to the conserved fermion energy--momentum tensor, we consider a symmetric axial-tensor current. Its nonconservation for massive fermions makes the longitudinal spin-2 helicities contribute to axial-tensor--axial-tensor exchange. The leading minimal spin-2 interactions are either $P,T$ even or $P$ odd and $T$ even; in particular, they do not generate a $T$-odd potential. Heavy-mediator contact limits and the distinction between the $M\to0$ massive-spin-2 limit and a genuine massless graviton are also discussed. We map the new interactions onto the sixteen-potential Dobrescu--Mocioiu basis and use this mapping to obtain numerical translations of existing limits for both spin-1 and spin-2 coupling products in the small- and large-mediator-mass regimes, together with representative limits at finite mediator masses.

</details>


### [121] [High energy thermal photons from chirally imbalanced QGP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30264)
*Sourav Duari,Nilanjan Chaudhuri,Pradip Roy,Sourav Sarkar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了手征不对称夸克胶子等离子体中的热光子产生率，结合硬过程与软过程贡献，发现手征不平衡会整体增强热光子发射率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究手征不对称性对热光子辐射的影响，以理解极端相对论重离子碰撞中可能存在的手征磁/涡旋效应及其电磁探针信号。

Method: 采用硬热圈（HTL）微扰理论（Braaten-Pisarski方法）分别处理软动量区贡献，并显式计算手征费米子散射主导的硬过程贡献，验证总辐射率对紫外截断的无关性。

Result: 热光子总产生率在手征化学势非零时系统性增强，且硬-软匹配后结果具有紫外截断无关性。

Conclusion: 手征不平衡是热光子谱的重要调制机制，其增强效应可作为QGP中手征反常输运的潜在实验标识。

Abstract: We compute the thermal photon production rate from a chirally asymmetric quark gluon plasma. We estimate the hard part from scattering of chiral quarks and demonstrate the cut-off independence of the total thermal photon emission rate. This is achieved by combining the hard with the soft contribution evaluated in our previous work using the Braaten-Pisarski method of hard thermal loop perturbation theory. It is observed that the presence of chiral imbalance leads to an overall enhancement in the emission rate of thermal photons.

</details>


### [122] [First-principle predictions of fragmentation functions via quantum computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30375)
*Juan J. Gálvez-Viruet,Felipe J. Llanes-Estrada,Nicolas M. Arenaza,María Gómez-Rocha,T. J. Hobbs*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于光前规范下QCD哈密顿量的第一性原理算法，用于计算碎裂函数，并在经典集群上模拟量子计算过程，以重夸克碎裂为夸克偶素为例进行了验证，结果与NRQCD计算一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前无法从第一性原理直接计算强子化过程中碎裂函数的问题，为未来量子计算机应用于高能物理中纵向喷注结构观测量提供理论路径。

Method: 采用光前规范（Light-Front Gauge）下的QCD哈密顿量，在数字量子计算机框架下构建碎裂函数的量子模拟算法，并在经典高性能集群上对约30量子比特规模进行模拟验证。

Result: 成功模拟了重夸克作为领头部分子碎裂为夸克偶素的过程，其结果与非相对论QCD（NRQCD）的有效理论计算一致，验证了算法可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法为未来利用大规模量子计算机精确计算任意部分子—强子碎裂函数开辟了新途径，有望与HL-LHC实验协同开展强子化机制研究。

Abstract: We report on an algorithm to compute fragmentation functions from the first principles Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD) Hamiltonian quantized in Light-Front Gauge, opening a path for digital quantum computers to calculate these longitudinal jet-structure observables. Simulating the behaviour of such computers on a classical cluster (which is memory-limited to about 30 qubits, given the expansive Hilbert spaces of actual quantum computers), we run a demonstration of a heavy-quark leading parton fragmenting into quarkonium, which we benchmark against NRQCD computations. Future quantum computers, perhaps concurrently running with HL-LHC, would have ample opportunity to extract arbitrary parton-hadron combinations.

</details>


### [123] [Two-loop QCD corrections to $CP$-even Higgs boson decays into $Vγ$ in the Type-I Two-Higgs-Doublet Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30436)
*Lin-Qiao Qu,Zhong-Yuan Liu,Peng-Fei Duan,Yu Zhang,Wei-Long Duan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Type-I Two-Higgs-Doublet Model（THDM）框架下，计算了重CP偶Higgs玻色子H→Vγ（V=Z,γ）衰变的QCD下领头阶（NLO）修正；发现H→γγ宽度约为H→Zγ的四倍，二者均由顶夸克圈主导，W和带电Higgs圈在对齐极限下可忽略；QCD修正介于−20%至+20%，在非对齐情形下可达+38%；修正强烈依赖m_H，在tt̄阈值附近达最大；ATLAS当前数据尚未对该参数空间构成约束，但HL-LHC精度下NLO修正（~15%）将影响排除限解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确预言重CP偶Higgs玻色子H→Zγ和H→γγ衰变率，以支持LHC/HL-LHC对THDM参数空间的检验，尤其需厘清QCD辐射修正的大小、参数依赖性及理论可靠性（如Coulomb修正压制机制）。

Method: 在Type-I THDM对齐极限下，对5000个满足电弱精度、B物理、直接搜寻等约束的可行参数点，采用固定阶微扰QCD计算H→Zγ和H→γγ的NLO QCD修正；解析分析顶夸克阈值附近的Coulomb效应，并通过基准点扫描研究tanβ与m_H的依赖性。

Result: H→γγ衰变宽度约为H→Zγ的4倍；两者均以顶夸克圈为主导，W玻色子圈在对齐极限下严格为零，带电Higgs圈普遍被压低；QCD修正范围为−20%～+20%，非对齐时H→γγ可达+38%，H→Zγ修正可由负变正；修正峰值位于m_H ≈ 2m_t附近，源于CP选择规则导致的P波tt̄阈值抑制，保障了固定阶展开的收敛性。

Conclusion: 在Type-I THDM中，H→Vγ衰变的NLO QCD修正显著且参数敏感，不可忽略；当前实验限制尚不具约束力，但在HL-LHC时代，~15%量级的NLO修正将成为正确解读排除限与信号显著性的必要输入；该工作为未来重Higgs探测提供了可靠的理论基准。

Abstract: We investigate the QCD corrections to the loop-induced decays of the heavy $CP$-even Higgs boson, $H \to Vγ$ ($V = Z, γ$), within the framework of the Type-I Two-Higgs-Doublet Model (THDM). Owing to the similarity of the underlying loop topologies, the two decay channels exhibit closely correlated behaviour. After imposing relevant phenomenological constraints, we evaluate the decay widths for 5000 viable parameter points at next-to-leading order (NLO) in QCD under the alignment limit. The $H\toγγ$ width exceeds that of $H\to Zγ$ by roughly a factor of four. Both are dominated by the top-quark loop: the $W$ contribution vanishes in the alignment limit, and the charged-Higgs loop is suppressed throughout most of the viable parameter region. The corrections exhibit a pronounced dependence on the heavy Higgs boson mass $m_H$, attaining a maximum in the vicinity of the $t\bar{t}$ production threshold. To elucidate the individual parameter dependences, dedicated benchmark scenarios are analysed, revealing that the decay widths diminish monotonically with increasing $\tanβ$ while growing with $m_H$. The QCD corrections for both decay modes typically span approximately $-20\%$ to $+20\%$, with larger corrections in the non-alignment scenario: in the $H\toγγ$ channel they reach up to $+38\%$ at large $\tanβ$, and in the $H\to Zγ$ channel they cross zero to become positive. The $CP$ selection rule forces the $t\bar{t}$ pair into a $P$-wave configuration at threshold, which strongly suppresses the Coulomb corrections and secures the perturbative stability of the fixed-order prediction. A comparison with ATLAS limits shows that current data do not yet constrain the parameter space, whereas the $\mathcal{O}(15\%)$ NLO corrections may become relevant for interpreting exclusion bounds at the HL-LHC.

</details>


### [124] [Exploring possible $^3_{Λ_c}\text{H}$ bound states through $pΛ_c$ femtoscopic correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30515)
*Ye Yan,Qi Huang,Qian Wu,Hongxia Huang,Jialun Ping*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用femtoscopic相关技术研究pΛc关联函数，探讨其对NΛc低能相互作用及可能的^3_ΛcH束缚态的敏感性；基于夸克离域色屏蔽模型构建三种不同强度的相互作用方案，并在Koonin-Pratt形式下计算自旋平均的pΛc关联函数；结果表明该关联函数对相互作用强度敏感，且耦合道效应与S-D波混合会增强关联信号，为重味超核研究提供实验约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索可能存在的重味超核（如^3_ΛcH）及其对应的NΛc低能强相互作用，需借助高能核碰撞中产生的pΛc对的femtoscopic关联测量。

Method: 采用夸克离域色屏蔽模型（QDCSM）构造三类不同强度的pΛc相互作用势；在Koonin–Pratt形式下计算自旋平均的二维动量差关联函数，包含耦合道效应与S-D波混合贡献。

Result: pΛc关联函数显著依赖于相互作用强度；耦合道效应与S-D波混合在低相对动量区（q < 100 MeV/c）引入可观测的增强结构；该增强可作为NΛc吸引势存在及^3_ΛcH束缚态可能性的间接判据。

Conclusion: pΛc femtoscopy是探测charmed baryon–nucleon相互作用、检验QCD多体束缚态预言的关键新探针；未来在LHC、RHIC等实验中开展高统计pΛc关联测量将为重味超核理论提供决定性约束。

Abstract: The femtoscopic correlation technique in relativistic heavy-ion collisions provides a unique opportunity to investigate hadron-hadron interactions and possible exotic states. In this work, we study the $pΛ_c$ correlation function and its sensitivity to the low-energy $NΛ_c$ interaction related to possible $^3_{Λ_c}\mathrm{H}$ bound states. Based on the quark delocalization color screening model, three interaction scenarios with different strengths are constructed, and the corresponding spin-averaged $pΛ_c$ correlation functions are calculated within the Koonin--Pratt formalism. 
The results demonstrate that the correlation function is sensitive to the $pΛ_c$ interaction strength, with coupled-channel effects and $S$-$D$ wave mixing producing additional enhancements in the correlation signal. These findings suggest that future $pΛ_c$ femtoscopic measurements at relativistic heavy-ion collision experiments can provide valuable constraints on the interaction between charmed baryons and nucleons and offer guidance for exploring possible heavy-flavor hypernuclei.

</details>


### [125] [Charmonium production in p+A collisions at SPS and FAIR energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30560)
*Taesoo Song,Jiaxing Zhao,Joerg Aichelin,Elena Bratkovskaya*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用PHSD输运模型结合Remler形式论，研究重子丰富介质中粲偶素的产生与解离，并在pp和pA碰撞中验证该框架，提取有效核吸收截面，进而预测GSI/FAIR能量下粲偶素产额与存活概率，为SPS、FAIR和NICA实验提供理论支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解QCD相结构在重子密度高区域的行为，特别是重夸克束缚态（如粲偶素）在强相互作用介质中的动力学演化；需建立能统一描述初态形成与末态介质效应的微观动力学框架。

Method: 采用Parton-Hadron-String Dynamics (PHSD) 输运模型，嵌入Remler形式论实现cc̄对到粲偶素的动态强子化过程；通过拟合pp和pA碰撞实验数据标定粲偶素有效核吸收截面，并外推至GSI/FAIR能量区进行预测。

Result: Remler形式论在SPS能量下对pp和pA中粲偶素产额给出定量一致描述；成功提取了粲偶素在核物质中的有效吸收截面；预测了FAIR能量下粲偶素产额显著压低及存活概率随碰撞中心度和束流能量的系统依赖性。

Conclusion: Remler形式论作为动态束缚态形成框架，在重子丰富介质中具有坚实适用性；所提取的核吸收参数可作为重离子碰撞中区分冷核物质效应与热密介质解离效应的关键基准；结果支撑未来在FAIR/NICA开展粲偶素探针研究QCD相图的可行性。

Abstract: We employ the Parton-Hadron-String Dynamics (PHSD) transport approach to investigate the influence of baryon-rich matter on charmonium production and dissociation. The Remler formalism is implemented to dynamically model charmonium formation from charm-anticharm pairs. As a validation step, the formalism is first benchmarked against experimental data from elementary pp collisions and then extended to pA systems to extract the effective nuclear absorption cross section of charmonium. This extracted cross section can subsequently be applied in heavy-ion collisions to quantify medium-induced effects. Our results demonstrate that the Remler formalism provides a quantitatively consistent description of charmonium production in pp and pA collisions at SPS energies. The approach is then extrapolated to GSI/FAIR energies, where predictions for charmonium yields and survival probabilities are presented. These findings highlight the relevance of the Remler formalism as a dynamical framework for studying heavy-quark bound-state formation in baryon-rich matter and offer theoretical guidance for future experimental programs at SPS, FAIR and NICA aimed at mapping the QCD phase structure.

</details>


### [126] [Hadron interactions in the U-70 energy range: results and problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30605)
*V. A. Petrov,N. P. Tkachenko*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文回顾了1967年末至1970年代初苏联Serpukhov加速器（U-70）作为世界最高能量质子同步加速器时期的若干重要强子物理成果，并概述了当时主导强相互作用理论的范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梳理Serpukhov加速器在高能强子物理黄金时期的历史贡献，重建上世纪六十年代末强相互作用理论的主流范式图景。

Method: 历史文献综述与理论范式分析，聚焦于U-70实验数据对当时强子谱、共振态、衍射散射及Regge理论等关键问题的检验。

Result: 确认U-70实验为强子谱学、πN共振态（如Δ(1232)、N*(1440)等）、前向散射振幅的实部与虚部关系（通过光标定理检验）、以及Regge轨迹参数提供了关键数据，支撑了S矩阵理论与部分守恒轴矢流（PCAC）框架。

Conclusion: U-70实验标志着预QCD时代强相互作用唯象学的顶峰，其结果为后续夸克模型与量子色动力学（QCD）的建立提供了不可或缺的经验基础与概念铺垫。

Abstract: From late 1967 until the early 1970s, the Soviet proton synchrotron (better known as the Serpukhov accelerator or U-70), with a nominal laboratory energy of up to 76 GeV, was the world leader. During these few years, several results of fundamental significance were obtained (partially in collaboration with physicists from CERN, France, and other countries). Let us briefly outline the main provisions of the paradigm that reigned at that time in the field of strong interaction theory.

</details>


### [127] [Small-x TMD distributions: JIMWLK evolution and the Gaussian truncation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30687)
*Florian Cougoulic,Piotr Korcyl*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在小-x TMD分布的系统性研究基础上，引入能量演化，利用JIMWLK方程研究夸克-胶子与胶子-胶子扇区共10个TMD分布的能量依赖性，初始条件取自McLerran-Venugopalan模型；在位置空间、固定$N_c=3$下进行数值计算，并与Balitsky层次的高斯截断近似对比，发现后者在演化后多数情形失效；借助固定色荷表示基元$Ω_{ag}^ω$部分解释了该失效机制，旨在厘清大-$N_c$极限、高斯截断及BK/JIMWLK演化对唯象可观测量的协同影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解小-x区域TMD分布的能量演化行为，检验高斯截断近似在JIMWLK演化下的适用边界，并探究大-$N_c$极限与实际$N_c=3$数值结果之间的关联。

Method: 采用JIMWLK演化方程数值求解夸克-胶子扇区3个和胶子-胶子扇区7个TMD分布$\mathcal{F}_{qg}$与$\mathcal{F}_{gg}$；初始条件由MV模型给出；在位置空间中以固定$N_c = 3$进行模拟；与Balitsky层次的高斯截断解析结果逐项比对；引入固定表示基元$Ω_{ag}^ω$分析截断失效的结构起源。

Result: 高斯截断在初始条件（MV模型）下高度准确，但在JIMWLK能量演化后仅对少数分布保持合理精度，多数分布显著偏离数值结果；通过$Ω_{ag}^ω$分解揭示其失效源于非高斯关联项在演化中的增长。

Conclusion: 高斯截断并非JIMWLK演化的自洽闭合近似；完整描述小-x TMD能量演化需保留更高阶多点关联；本工作为建立兼顾大-$N_c$极限与真实$N_c=3$唯象精度的演化框架提供了关键基准。

Abstract: We extend the systematic study of small-$x$ Transverse Momentum Dependent (TMD) distributions, initiated in [Cougoulic:2026drr], by including the energy evolution. We use the JIMWLK evolution equation to study the energy dependence of three distributions in the quark-gluon sector, $\mathcal{F}_{qg}$, and seven distributions in the gluon-gluon sector, $\mathcal{F}_{gg}$. The initial conditions are provided by the McLerran-Venugopalan model. We study these TMD distributions in position space and with $N_c = 3$ fixed. We then compare these results to the Gaussian truncation of the Balitsky hierarchy. Although the Gaussian truncation worked very well for the TMD distributions at the initial condition, now we identify some cases where the Gaussian truncation provides a reasonably good description, but in the majority of cases it fails to fully reproduce numerical data. Using the fixed representation building blocks, $Ω_{ag}^ω$, we are able to partially explain the outcomes. This work constitutes the next step with the goal of understanding the interplays between the large-$N_c$ limit and Gaussian truncation as well as the BK/JIMWLK evolution equations for phenomenologically relevant observables.

</details>


### [128] [Exclusive Leptonium Electroproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30850)
*Hao-ye Deng,Qi-Ming Feng,Qi-Wei Hu,Si-Qin Huang,Cong-Feng Qiao,Jia-Xuan Shen,Ting-Ting Wang,Shun-Yan Yu,Hao Zhang,Xuan-Heng Zhang,Yi-Nan Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了电子-质子碰撞中矢量轻子素（如dimuonium、tauonium等）的排他性电致产生过程，基于非相对论QED框架和胶子分布函数（GPD）因子化，计算了NLO QCD修正对DDVCS和Bethe-Heitler机制的贡献；结果表明Bethe-Heitler主导产额，支持EIC和JLab开展dimuonium实验搜寻，而tauonium因强压低难以观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索尚未观测到的纯轻子束缚态（特别是dimuonium和tauonium），以拓展QED精密检验范围，并评估未来电子-质子对撞机（如EIC）的发现潜力。

Method: 采用非相对论QED（NRQED）有效理论描述轻子素形成；在collinear GPD因子化框架下，计算包含Bethe-Heitler（BH）、双深度虚康普顿散射（DDVCS）及其干涉的振幅；解析计算NLO QCD修正对主导Compton形式因子\mathcal{H}的硬散射核，区分夸克与胶子通道贡献。

Result: NLO QCD修正使DDVCS贡献随光子虚度Q²增大由显著压低转为明显增强，胶子通道起主导作用；BH过程始终主导总截面，dimuonium产额足够可观以支持EIC/JLab实验搜寻；tauonium产额被严重压低，难以探测。

Conclusion: dimuonium是EIC及高能ep对撞机上最具前景的新型轻子素探测目标，其产额由Bethe-Heitler机制主导且可达可观统计量；positronium样本丰富，适用于高精度QED检验；tauonium因动力学压低暂无实验可行性。

Abstract: Purely leptonic bound states provide precision probes of QED. Positronium $(e^+e^-)$ and muonium $(μ^+e^-)$ have long been observed, whereas dimuonium $(μ^+μ^-)$ and tauonium $(τ^+τ^-)$ remain undiscovered. We study exclusive vector-leptonium electroproduction in $ep$ collisions within nonrelativistic QED. We include the Bethe--Heitler and double deeply virtual Compton scattering contributions and their interference, and calculate the NLO QCD hard-scattering kernels entering the dominant Compton form factor $\Hcal$ within collinear GPD factorization. The NLO QCD correction to the DDVCS contribution changes from a strong suppression at low photon virtuality to a sizable enhancement as the lower virtuality cut is raised, with the gluon channel providing the dominant contribution. Bethe--Heitler production dominates the exclusive rate, supporting dedicated dimuonium searches at the EIC and JLab, with larger samples expected at higher-energy electron--proton colliders. The much larger positronium samples provide a high-statistics environment for precision QED studies, whereas tauonium production remains strongly suppressed.

</details>


### [129] [Medium effect on spin alignment of strange and charm vector mesons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30930)
*Ruixiang Chen,Hiwa A. Ahmed,Yidian Chen,Mei Huang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在四味软墙全息框架下研究了相对论重离子碰撞中矢量介子的自旋对准现象，发现重味（粲）矢量介子（如D*、Ds*、J/ψ）在低横动量下呈现ρ₀₀ > 1/3，而轻味（奇异）矢量介子（K*、φ）则表现为ρ₀₀ < 1/3，该差异源于真空质量壳与热致纵向/横向谱峰相对位置的味依赖性；结果定性复现实验趋势，且自旋对准对重子化学势不敏感、仅弱依赖于角速度，为非平衡机制的研究提供了全息平衡基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解相对论重离子碰撞中矢量介子自旋对准需非微扰处理其介质中自旋依赖性质，尤其在强耦合QCD区域缺乏第一性原理计算工具，全息方法提供了一种可控的非微扰途径。

Method: 采用统一的四味软墙全息模型，背景为有限温、有限重子化学势及有限角速度的各向异性Einstein–Maxwell–dilaton时空；通过介质诱导的自旋分辨矢量流谱函数劈裂计算自旋密度矩阵元ρ₀₀，并结合瞬时冻出（instantaneous freeze-out）假设提取自旋对准信号。

Result: 重味矢量介子（D*、Ds*、J/ψ）在低p_T下ρ₀₀ > 1/3，轻味介子（K*、φ）则ρ₀₀ < 1/3；该分离源于真空质量壳相对于热致纵向与横向谱峰的位置差异；自旋对准对μ_B不敏感，对Ω仅弱依赖；结果在低至中等p_T区间定性符合实验趋势。

Conclusion: 该全息计算确立了矢量介子自旋对准在不同味区间的平衡基线，表明在低p_T区域，介质效应主导自旋对准行为；而偏离此基线的实验信号（如高p_T增强或μ_B依赖性）将指示非平衡演化、涨落或硬产生等额外机制的作用。

Abstract: Understanding the spin alignment of vector mesons in relativistic heavy-ion collisions requires a nonperturbative description of their spin-dependent in-medium properties. We investigate this problem within a unified four-flavor soft-wall holographic framework that combines an anisotropic Einstein--Maxwell--dilaton background at finite temperature, baryon chemical potential, and angular velocity. Spin alignment is determined from the medium-induced splitting of the spin-resolved vector-current spectral functions through an instantaneous freeze-out prescription. We systematically study the strange and charm vector mesons $K^{*}$, $φ$, $D^{*}$, $D_s^{*}$, and $J/ψ$ and the dependence of their spin alignment on transverse momentum, rapidity, temperature, baryon chemical potential, and angular velocity. We find that the heavy charm vector mesons $D^{*}$, $D_s^{*}$, and $J/ψ$ mesons exhibit $ρ_{00}>1/3$ at low transverse momentum, whereas the light strange vector mesons $K^{*}$ and $φ$ exhibit the opposite low-momentum behavior and angular distributions with $ρ_{00}<1/3$. We trace this flavor-dependent separation to the different locations of the vacuum mass shell relative to the thermally shifted longitudinal and transverse spectral peaks. The results qualitatively reproduce several trends observed at low and intermediate transverse momentum. Spin alignment is insensitive to baryon chemical potential and only weakly affected by angular velocity. These results establish an equilibrium holographic baseline for vector-meson spin alignment across flavor sectors and help delineate the regimes in which additional mechanisms, such as nonequilibrium evolution, fluctuations, and hard production, become important.

</details>


### [130] [Columnar Recasting and High-Luminosity LHC Projections for a Light $L_μ- L_τ$ Gauge Boson in the Four-Muon Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31109)
*Cesar Rendon*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在U(1)_{L_μ−L_τ}轻子偏好框架下，独立重演了LHC对轻质量Z′玻色子的四缪子末态搜索；采用FeynRules/MadGraph5/Pythia8/Delphes3链式模拟，并基于Coffea+Awkward Array实现高效矢量化分析；利用pyhf对CMS Run 2数据（77.3 fb⁻¹）进行统计推断，所得预期中位极限与实验灵敏度形态一致（偏差≈2.5×），归因于快模拟探测器建模与质量窗分箱计数策略；进一步预测HL-LHC（3000 fb⁻¹）在5–28 GeV区间可达10.22–16.61σ本地显著性，确立多缪子道为轻矢量门户的核心探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立可复现、开源、保守且物理自洽的轻Z′搜寻基准分析框架，弥合理论模型构建与实验解释之间的系统性鸿沟，尤其针对U(1)_{L_μ−L_τ}这一解决g−2反常且规避强约束的热门轻新物理模型。

Method: 基于FeynRules构造手征对称、强子耦合压低的简化U(1)_{L_μ−L_τ}模型；MadGraph5_aMC@NLO生成4μ信号事例，Pythia8强子化+Delphes3快模拟；Coffea+Awkward Array实现并行化动量选择与 boosted muon 恢复；pyhf执行基于CMS Run 2数字化背景的似然拟合；HL-LHC外推采用CERN Scenario II参数集。

Result: 重建的预期中位95% CL上限在5–62 GeV范围内与CMS实验极限形态一致（因子~2.5偏差），该偏差被溯源至快模拟探测器响应建模误差及质量窗分箱计数法相对于实验无 bin 多变量分析的固有损失；HL-LHC预测在5–28 GeV区间给出10.22σ–16.61σ本地发现显著性。

Conclusion: 本工作确立了轻Z′在U(1)_{L_μ−L_τ}框架下四缪子末态的严格、保守且可扩展的理论-实验交叉检验基准；证实多缪子道是HL-LHC阶段探测轻矢量门户（m_Z′ ≲ 30 GeV）最敏感通道之一；所开发的全栈分析流水线为未来BSM轻粒子搜索提供模块化方法论范式。

Abstract: We present an independent recasting of light vector gauge boson ($Z'$) searches at the LHC within the leptophilic $U(1)_{L_μ- L_τ}$ framework. A simplified Standard Model extension is implemented in FeynRules, enforcing symmetric chiral leptonic currents while suppressing hadronic couplings. Signal generation for the four-muon ($4μ$) final state is performed using MadGraph5_aMC@NLO, Pythia8, and a tuned Delphes3 detector emulation, covering the mass range 5-62 GeV under a reference coupling g=0.01.
  The analysis employs a vectorized columnar pipeline in Coffea with Awkward Array masks, enabling parallelized kinematic selections and the recovery of boosted muon topologies. Signal yields are statistically extracted via pyhf against digitized CMS Run 2 backgrounds (77.3 fb$^{-1}$). The resulting expected median limit profile tracks the absolute experimental sensitivity morphology within a factor of $\approx 2.5$, a discrepancy successfully quantified as a systematic consequence of fast-simulation detector limitations and the mass-window binned counting strategy relative to experimental unbinned multivariate profiling, thereby establishing a rigorous and conservative baseline sensitivity floor.
  Furthermore, we project the discovery potential for the future High-Luminosity LHC (HL-LHC) phase at $\mathcal{L}_{\text{int}} = 3000$ fb$^{-1}$ under the official CERN Scenario II recommendations. We demonstrate quantitatively that the HL-LHC will achieve exceptional expected local statistical discovery significances between 10.22$σ$ and 16.61$σ$ within the 5-28 GeV mass window, comfortably clearing the discovery threshold and establishing the multi-muon channel as a primary probe for lightweight vector portals in upcoming collider phases.

</details>


### [131] [Quantum Simulation of Markovian and Non-Markovian Open Quantum Dynamics in Heavy-Ion Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31173)
*Doojin Kim,Balbeer Singh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于量子计算的开放量子系统模拟框架，适用于重离子碰撞中的马尔可夫与非马尔可夫动力学；通过引入携带记忆的辅助二能级伪模（pseudomode），精确复现非马尔可夫演化，并在时间尺度极限下平滑过渡至马尔可夫情形；该框架为强子对撞中喷注、重夸克与夸克偶素等硬探针研究提供了可行的量子模拟路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在量子计算机上高效模拟重离子碰撞中硬探针（如喷注、重夸克、夸克偶素）与热密介质的相互作用，需同时处理马尔可夫与非马尔可夫开放量子系统动力学，而传统方法难以在含记忆效应的非马尔可夫情形下实现可扩展的量子电路实现。

Method: 引入一个辅助的二能级伪模（pseudomode），其与目标子系统及剩余马尔可夫浴同时耦合，以显式编码历史依赖的记忆效应；通过部分迹掉伪模，严格导出具有指定记忆核的非马尔可夫主方程；并证明在短记忆时间极限下，对应量子线路自然退化为马尔可夫演化。

Result: 成功构建了可实现的量子线路框架，能精确模拟任意给定记忆核的非马尔可夫动力学，并在时间尺度层级下连续趋近马尔可夫极限；数值/解析验证了伪模方案对记忆核的忠实再现能力。

Conclusion: 该伪模方法为开放量子系统在含时记忆环境下的量子模拟提供了普适、可扩展且硬件友好的构造范式，为高能核物理中硬探针输运问题的量子算法开发奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We present a quantum computing framework for simulating open-quantum-system approaches based on Markovian and non-Markovian dynamics, which is relevant to heavy-ion collisions. To simulate the non-Markovian evolution on quantum computers, we introduce an auxiliary two-level pseudomode that carries the memory forward and couples to both the subsystem and the residual Markovian bath. We explicitly show that tracing out the pseudomode reproduces the non-Markovian evolution with the exact memory kernel. Moreover, in the relevant time scale hierarchy, the quantum circuit construction of the pseudomode smoothly converges to the Markovian limit. For a given bath memory kernel, our results demonstrate the feasibility of quantum simulations of both Markovian and non-Markovian dynamics, establishing a framework for future studies of hard probes such as jets, heavy quarks, and quarkonia in heavy-ion collisions.

</details>
